HomeDepotsSafety

Depot Operations Manual

Harbourline Freight Depot Operations Manual. This manual describes every depot in the Harbourline Freight network. Each depot has its own section, identified by a depot code, which names the depot manager and sets out the canteen hours, staff shuttle, parking, fire assembly point, dock doors, same-day dispatch cut-off, cold storage range and security desk extension for that depot. This edition covers 748 depots.

1. Quiet Heights depot (HD-00001)

The Quiet Heights depot (HD-00001) in Singapore, Singapore is managed by Nadia Ibrahim, who is the first point of contact for access passes, building issues and local safety arrangements. New joiners receive a site induction on their first morning, before they are allowed onto the warehouse floor.

The canteen at HD-00001 is open from 07:45 to 20:45 on working days and accepts the staff meal card. A free shuttle bus leaves the nearest station at 06:15 for the early shift and again eight hours later for the late shift.

There are 80 parking spaces at HD-00001, allocated on a first-come basis; those nearest the entrance are reserved for visitors and for colleagues with reduced mobility. If the fire alarm sounds, leave by the nearest exit and go to the assembly point, which is the grass area beside the main gate.

The depot has 33 dock doors and 26 forklift trucks. The same-day dispatch cut-off at HD-00001 is 18:30; orders released to the floor after that time are dispatched on the next working day.

Cold storage is held at 15 to 25 degrees Celsius. The security desk at HD-00001 can be reached on extension 7058 at any hour, and safety shoes and a high-visibility vest must be worn at all times in the warehouse.

There are 265 parking spaces at HD-00745, allocated on a first-come basis; those nearest the entrance are reserved for visitors and for colleagues with reduced mobility. If the fire alarm sounds, leave by the nearest exit and go to the assembly point, which is the container yard entrance.

The depot has 39 dock doors and 20 forklift trucks. The same-day dispatch cut-off at HD-00745 is 18:00; orders released to the floor after that time are dispatched on the next working day.

Cold storage is held at 2 to 8 degrees Celsius. The security desk at HD-00745 can be reached on extension 8823 at any hour, and safety shoes and a high-visibility vest must be worn at all times in the warehouse.
